- **Task 0.2 (the spike) must land before** 4.x (verifier/metrics), 7.3–7.5 (conversion job, parity tolerance), 15.1 (the S6 latency numbers in §24) and 17.1 (Export_Image pins). It therefore uses **throwaway scripts against the rfdetr API only** (no `train_rfdetr_seg.py`, no `compare_rfdetr_parity` mask term, no production fixture; the existing `_common.cap_onnx_ir_version` and `checkpoint_probe.classify_checkpoint`, unchanged at 43cd4ff1, are the only repository code it runs) and its one optional input from this plan is the wave-1 converter of 3.2 for the COCO dataset — replaceable by a hand-built COCO RLE JSON. Its two runs exercise the three onnxruntime versions the Seg graph meets before a device: the Export_Image's own (1.30.0 at 43cd4ff1) and the Fleet_Floor_Runtime 1.16.3 in run (i), the trainer DLC's 1.19.2 in run (ii). Groups 1–3, 5, 6, 8, 9 (except the timing fixture), 11–14 do not depend on it; their provisional constants come from design.md §1/§4 and are re-checked in 0.3.
- **Test commands** (design "Testing strategy"): backend from `edge-cv-portal/backend` with `~/.venvs/dda-portal-tests/bin/python -m pytest tests/<file> -q -p no:cacheprovider` — targeted files only, never all of `tests/` in one process; device suites from the repo root with `python3 -m pytest test/backend-test/<file> -q -p no:cacheprovider`; Portal frontend (`edge-cv-portal/frontend`, vitest): `npx vitest run <file>` then `npm run build`; HMI (`src/frontend`, CRA/Jest — not vitest): `CI=true npm test -- --watchAll=false --testPathPattern=<pattern>` then `npm run build`; CDK: `npm run build` and `npm test -- <file>` in `edge-cv-portal/infrastructure`; drift guards and the full preservation suite per `.kiro/steering/builds.md`.
- **MCP pins** (Req 13.2): no tool is added, so the Portal counts 89 / 88 / 31 (`tests/mcp/test_mcp_protocol.py:108`, `test_mcp_catalog_classification.py:136`, `test_mcp_guardrails.py:63`) and the edge counts 73 / 47 (`test/backend-test/mcp_edge/test_property_child_catalog.py:184, 229`, `test_child_protocol.py:99`) hold; the version literal moves in three places (13.2: `mcp_portal/__init__.py:9`, the eight `tests/mcp` sites, and the CDK constant `mcp-stack.ts:113` that the deployed Lambda actually reports — C15) and the edge path-grammar pins move (10.7).
- **Deploy rules** (Req 17.7, `.kiro/steering/builds.md`): never run a portal deploy while a component build is running (`pgrep -af "gdk component build"`, `pgrep -af build-custom.sh`); move `edge-cv-portal/infrastructure/cdk.out` aside and run the guards green first; one LocalServer build at a time. **Deploy flag-less** — do **not** pass `-c deployGroundedSamWorker=false`: since `portal-deploy-flag-hardening` that explicit `false` is the deliberate teardown path and deletes the live Grounded-SAM worker (`compute-stack.ts:3141-3160`, `context-helpers.ts:11-20`; `detector-checkpoint-import/tasks.md:21`), which Req 2.4 and the Req 18.2(a) labeling run need. Requirement 17.7 and the design's "Gates" sentence say otherwise and are corrected by task 0.1 (C13).
- GPU work (spike job, the Req 18 training run) uses `ml.g4dn.xlarge` (`DETECTION_DEFAULT_INSTANCE_TYPE`); the account quota is one concurrent instance — serialise. Conversion jobs are CPU. Tasks 0.2, 17–23 need real AWS and, for 21–22, hardware: skip them in unattended harness runs.

- **New-field preservation ledger (BRIEF convergence note 3, item 1; Requirement 17.1; 2026-11-22, read-only at 43cd4ff1).** Every key, column, env var, manifest / metadata / record / response field this plan ADDS to a surface a YOLO or RF-DETR detection model also produces, with its verdict: **Seg-only** = written only when the Seg path produced it (the owning task states the condition) · **every model** = written regardless (justified). Format `surface — key(s): verdict (task; condition or justification; pins)`. **Device.** Triton `anomalies` Detection_List entries — `class_index`, `mask_rle`, `mask_area`: Seg-only (10.1; contributed by `InstanceSegmentationResult.serialize()`, never by a `DetectionResult`; `test_lfv_detection_tensor_set.py` holds); `mask_truncated: True`: Seg-only (10.1; set only on an entry whose `mask_rle` was dropped by the ceiling). Triton `mask` tensor: Seg-only (10.1; painted only when `instances` is non-empty, else the empty mask of `:364`). Capture record (marshal) — `anomaly-mask-ref-detected` + `-metadata`, `<id>.mask.png`: Seg-only (10.2; `is_detection and _has_anomaly_mask`); detections block / sidecar `mask_rle`, `mask_area`, `mask_truncated`: Seg-only (10.2; copied when present); `detections_truncated: True`: Seg-only (10.2; only when an entry was truncated — C21); `mask_color_map`: Seg-only (10.2; only when a mask was written and `_MASK_UTILS`); `class_index`: not new (`:453` writes it today); pins `test_marshal_detections_block.py:187, 268, 357` hold. Live result dict (`save_image_object`) — per-entry keys: Seg-only (pass-through, 10.3); `detections_truncated`: Seg-only (10.3; copied only when the block carries it — C21; `test_detection_branch_output_is_unchanged :742-782` holds); `mask_image` / `mask_background` / `anomalies` on a detection result: Seg-only (10.3; only when `get_mask_base64_image` returns a mask). Device DB row — `detections` column: **every model** with a non-empty `detections` map (10.4; justified: Requirement 10.5(b) "this column is unconditional", Req 17.3 moves `OPTIONAL_FIELDS` / `LOADED_DEFAULTS` / `LIST_ITEM_FIELDS` / `:392-394`); `GET /workflows/{id}/results` items — `detections: null`: **every row** (10.4; same justification, `:1347` moves). `/overlay` body — `instances`, `detectionsTruncated`: **every run**, `null` for non-instance (10.5; justified: the fixed four-key shape of design §10(c) / C21 keeps the HMI type and the edge drop path static; `test_workflow_run_results_api.py:308` moves). Run metadata `_normalize_entry` — `mask_area`: Seg-only (10.5; only when the raw entry carries a finite number); `mask_rle`: never. HMI `RunDetection.maskArea` / `Mask area (px)` column / `LiveResultCard` "Mask coverage": Seg-only (10.8; only when present / some row has it / `labelInfoList` non-empty — a YOLO live result has no `anomalies`). Export DAO projections / accessor dicts — `detections`: **every exported row** (`None` for YOLO rows) (10.6; justified: reaches only the writer and the `captureIdPath` scratch file, never the classification manifest's named-field writer; the four `test_inference_result_accessor.py` exact dicts move, Req 17.3); instance manifest lines: Seg-only (rows with a `mask_rle` entry). Edge MCP (10.7): drops only, `dropped_fields` listed iff present — no new key. Device manifest — `detection.mask_threshold` / `output_mode` / `output_names`, `pixel_level_classes` on a detector manifest, omitted `task` (semantic mode): Seg-only (6.1 / 6.3; the `rf_detr_seg` block only; `test_detection_manifest_parity.py:165-168` holds; `output_mode=None` for `rf_detr`, 6.2).

Import record — `model_type` override, `detection` block: Seg-only (8.3; present only when the Internal_Invocation body carries them, which 8.2 does only for `is_seg` — this pass; `test_model_converter_fine_tunable.py:167` holds). `inspect_onnx_model` response — `num_queries`, `mask_grid`, `network_input`, `output_names`, `suggested_type = 'instance_segmentation'`: Seg-only (8.1; the `n_out >= 3` branch; the two-output and PyTorch branches are unchanged); `disabled_reason`: Seg-only and OFF-only (8.1; C4). `convert_model` response — `output_mode`, `deprecation`: Seg-only / alias-only (8.2, this pass). `get_supported_types` — `model_types.instance_segmentation`, `common_dimensions.instance_segmentation`: **every call** when the switch is ON (8.1; justified: a type listing enumerates every type by construction, omitted when OFF; no caller or pin at 43cd4ff1). `GET /captures` page — per-capture `detections_truncated`, `has_masks`, `jsonl_key`: **every capture record** (11.1; justified: the light-page contract of design §11 / C10 — the viewer keys the detail fetch on `has_masks && jsonl_key` and the flags are `False` / absent-safe for a YOLO or anomaly capture; additive on a surface Requirement 17.1 does not list; `tests/test_captures.py` asserts single keys, `:362-366, :384, :455-458`; `ResultsViewer.Capture` fields optional); per-entry `mask_area`, `mask_truncated`: Seg-only (11.1; when present); `mask_rle`: detail request only. `portal_data_captures_list` (MCP) output: the same page, same three flags, same justification (≈ 40 B per capture under `MAX_RESULT_BYTES`; the Portal catalog schema is unchanged, 13.1). Workflow version item — `has_rfdetr_seg_model`: **every workflow** (14.4; justified: mirrors `has_stream_features` / `has_llm_inference`, written unconditionally at `workflow_packaging.py:2928-2948` so re-packaging clears a stale `True`; read with `.get(...)` → `False`; not a Requirement 17.1 surface; no pin — `git grep 'UpdateExpression|:hsf|has_stream_features' -- tests` finds only fixture writers). `manifest.json` floors: no new key; values raised only when `rfdetr_seg=True` (14.4; `GOLDEN_MANIFEST_KEYS` holds). Deploy 409 details — `rfdetr_seg_model`, `rfdetr_seg_min_local_server_versions`: Seg-only (14.4, clarified this pass: only when `has_rfdetr_seg_model`, the `:4615-4623` pattern). Lambda environment — `PORTAL_RFDETR_SEG_ENABLED` on three Lambdas, `WORKFLOW_RFDETR_SEG_MIN_LOCAL_SERVER_VERSIONS` on two: **every deployment** (14.2 / 14.3; justified: the switch and the floor themselves, Req 15.1 / 14.8; never `lambdaEnvironment`, so the exact-key oracle holds). `config.json rfDetrSegEnabled`: **every frontend deployment** (14.2; justified: the switch's mirror, absent = ON). MCP schemas — `mask_threshold`, `output_mode` (convert), `output_mode` (training create) and the two enum values: schema inputs, optional, no output key (13.1; counts 89 / 88 / 31 hold). **Result:** every addition is either Seg-only with its condition stated in the owning task, or an "every model" write justified above; the pass-10 `packaged_output_mode` / `output_mode` write and the three unscoped writes (import payload, `convert_model` response, `training.py:688` / `:721`) were the only unconditional ones found and are now scoped.
- **Shape-based output identification ledger (BRIEF convergence note 3, item 2; `web-answers.md` WQ-req-author-3 pitfall: `dets` `[1,Q,4]` and `labels` `[1,Q,C+1]` are indistinguishable by shape when `C + 1 == 4`; 2026-11-22, read-only at 43cd4ff1).** Every site this plan touches or adds that tells the three Seg outputs apart, with the rule it applies — **names first when present (`dets` / `labels` / `masks`), shape as the fallback, the ambiguous nameless case fatal where a job or request can fail, graph order with a warning where the device must keep running (DD18) — and its three-class test case.** (1) `train_rfdetr_seg.verify_seg_outputs` (4.2): names settle `S == 4`; nameless two `[1, Q, 4]` → `FATAL … ambiguous`; `masks` = the only rank-4 output; test 4.4 ("the names tie-break, the nameless ambiguity"). (2) `export_checkpoint.verify_rfdetr_seg_contract` (7.3): delegates to (1) after the float32 check; test 7.6. (3) `export_checkpoint` `reference()` closure (7.3): no identification — the `forward_export` tuple is `(boxes, logits, masks)` by construction (WQ-design-author-3). (4) `export_checkpoint.compare_rfdetr_parity` (7.4, this pass): pairs by shape, then by position for equal shapes (`:634-636`), correct because the runtime outputs (`sess.run(None, …)` at `:422-424` and `:541`) and the reference tuple are both in graph order; `got_masks` = the only rank-4 array; test 7.6 (three-class pair). (5) `export_checkpoint.build_conversion_metadata` (7.3): takes `[boxes, logits, masks]` as (1) returns them and writes `onnx_output_names` in graph order — no identification of its own. (6) `detector_conversion.check_onnx_against_record` (7.2, this pass — tasks review pass 10, finding 1): shape (`s[2] == 4` boxes, other rank-3 logits, rank-4 masks); both rank-3 ending in 4 → `summary['output_names']` (`:1123`), falling back to output order like the detection `else`; test 7.6 (named, nameless, `rf_detr` three-class unchanged). (7) `detector_conversion.check_metadata_against_record` (7.2): compares `sorted()` shape lists and `top_k` — order-free, no identification. (8) `packaging.package_trained_detection_component` shape block (6.2, this pass): shape; both rank-3 ending in 4 → aligned `onnx_output_names`, else list order; never a 400 because the two shapes are equal; test 6.4 (three-class pick). (9) `model_converter.inspect_onnx_model` (8.1): shape; `S == 4` by names; nameless → `suggested_type = 'unknown'` + the ambiguity hint (the Smart Import 400 route, Req 8.1); test 8.4 ("three-class resolved by names"). (10) `model_converter.convert_model` (8.2, DD15): uses (9) and forwards `output_names` (graph order) into the manifest through `generate_dda_package` → `build_detection_device_manifest(output_names=…)` (6.3 / 6.1), which is what gives the device decoders their names. (11) `train_rfdetr_seg.build_seg_metadata` (4.3): `onnx_output_names` from `read_onnx_io` in graph order → `device_manifest_hints` → `detection.output_names` (6.2). (12) Device `RfDetrSegPostProcessor._split_outputs` (9.3, DD18): shape in any order; `S == 4` → `detection.output_names`, then list order with a one-time warning (the device never fatals at run time; `OnnxRunner.__call__` returns graph order, `inference_runtimes.py:386-389`); test 9.5. (13) Device `RfDetrDetectionPostProcessor._split_outputs` (9.4, DD18): the same three lines; test 9.5 (`test_rf_detr_detection_postprocessor.py` three-class case, Req 17.2). (14) `rf_detr_semantic_segmentation_postprocessor._split_outputs` (`:122-157` at 43cd4ff1: shape only, `last dim == num_classes` heuristic): replaced by the import shim to (12) (9.3). (15) Detection `train_rfdetr.verify_two_outputs` (`:566-580`, **untouched**, Req 17.1): names first, shape, nameless both-4 → order as given (`:577-580`) — the rule (6) and (8) mirror for their fallback. (16) Triton base / `OnnxRunner`: no identification — the runner's list goes to the decoder in graph order. (17) Spike 0.2 S2: records the names and dims as loaded by onnxruntime 1.30.0 / 1.16.3 / 1.19.2 — the fact that decides whether the name-first branch of (1), (6), (9), (12), (13) is ever exercised by a real 1.10.1 export (WQ-req-author-3 expects names to survive; `web-answers.md`). (18) Frontend: no site — `SmartImport.tsx` reads `num_classes` / `suggested_type` / `output_names` from (9). **Result:** 18 sites, each with one rule; (4), (6) and (8) were unstated or wrong before this pass (C30 amended, C33).

Group order is dependency order: groups 1–3 feed 4–8; 9 feeds 10; 10 and 11 share the Detection_List contract (§Data contracts); 12–14 consume the backend fields; 16 gates 17; 17 precedes 18 (no portal deploy during a build); 20 precedes 21–22 because 14.8(b)/(c) refuse a Seg package or deploy for an arch without a floor entry; 23 closes.

the two callers (`package_components` for trained records, `package_detector_conversion` for Conversion_Records) resolve the Seg mode **once**, through a pure module-level `effective_output_mode(det, hints=None, override=None)` = `override or det.get('output_mode') or (hints or {}).get('output_mode') or DEFAULT_OUTPUT_MODE`, pass it as `output_mode=mode` and persist that same value as `packaged_output_mode`; inside the function a given `output_mode` is used verbatim and only an absent one falls back to record → `device_manifest_hints` → default (the design's precedence, reachable only by a direct call), so the manifest's `detection.output_mode` and the record's `packaged_output_mode` cannot disagree; the shape block (`:502-510`) requires `len(real_shapes) == 3` for Seg (two entries → 400 `Seg record has a two-output artifact: <shapes>`), `stage0['output_shape'] = logits`, `stage0['output_shapes'] = [boxes, logits, masks]` — **and the guard is replaced, not just the length test** (tasks review pass 9, finding 2;
